💡 Advice 💡
============

Matmul Optimization
-------------------
 1401    0.1 %   SLOW  MatmulDeviceOperation 768 x 1024 x 1024                                             0        61 μs          1 μs     88   86 GB/s  16.9 %  26.5 TFLOPs   21.8 %  HiFi4 BF16 x BF16 => BF16
- If possible place input 0 in L1 (currently in DEV_1_DRAM_INTERLEAVED)
- in0_block_w=1 is small, try in0_block_w=2 or above
- HiFi2 may also work, it discards the lowest bit of the activations and has 2x the throughput of HiFi4

 1404    0.2 %   SLOW  MatmulDeviceOperation 768 x 1024 x 4096                                             0        97 μs          1 μs    104  167 GB/s  32.6 %  66.2 TFLOPs   46.0 %  HiFi4 BF16 x BF16 => BF16
